About the edition.

Edition. عصام موسى هادي, دار الصديق للنشر، الجبيل - السعودية, 2nd ed., 1435 AH / 2014 CE. The headings, report numbers and order follow the supplied Shamela text (book 1194).

Structure. The index preserves the edition’s 38 books, its chapter groups, and the chapter headings beneath them. Subheadings printed within a chapter appear before their reports.

Numbering. The reports retain their printed numbers, 1 to 4,341.

English translation. The supplied bilingual source includes English for all 4,341 reports and its editorial notes. Some entries are marked for review in the source metadata; the English is presented as supplied.

Footnotes. Notes from the edition are shown beside the first report from each printed page. Original Arabic and translated English are retained.
